Volhynian Marriages, 1880-85 (M. Momose)

        Published by the Odessa Digital Library - 12 Feb 2000
             http://www.odessa3.org

        This document may be freely used for personal, nonprofit
        purposes or linked by other WWW sites.  It may also be
        shared with others, provided the header with copyright
        notice is included.  However, it may not be republished
        in any form without permission of the copyright owner.

        Copyright 2000, Marcia Momose

           This file contains marriage records from the Parishes
           of Volhynia, South Russia for the period of 1880-85.
           This information was compiled by Marcia Momose and
           coworkers January, 2000 from the St. Petersburg
           Lutheran Evangelical archives published by the LDS
           and edited by Marty McMahon. Revised 3-12-00.

           Please take some time and review the General file
           in this Volhynia - St Petersburg directory to gain
           insight into the history and a list of the team
           working on this project.
